LISTEN TO BEST SOLO HINDI SONGS OF LATA MANGESHKAR



**LISTEN TO BEST SOLO HINDI SONGS OF LATA MANGESHKAR**

**Shrikant G. Talageri**



I have already put up a “**Listen** **To**” article featuring “**other** **female** **singers**” (i.e. other than **Asha** **Bhosle** and **Lata** **Mangeshkar**) and after that featuring the solo songs of **Asha** **Bhosle**. Now this is the last article on solo songs, featuring the solo songs of **Lata** **Mangeshkar**.



I think it is quite a *comprehensive* collection (though given the massive number of songs which must be there, it cannot be a *complete* one) of **Lata** **Mangeshkar’s** famous solo songs in Hindi. To many, it will seem as if beautiful lesser-known songs are missing, and to others, the lesser-known songs included may seem superfluous, but both reactions are inevitable depending on the viewer.
